# Official sign captions and accessibility text

**Step 4D — reviewed 2 October 2026.**

> This material is a learning aid to help understanding. It is not an official source and cannot replace current official information or applicable rules.

All English labels and explanations below are **editorial translations and original teaching text**, not official agency translations. Swedish names preserve the official page headings. Numbers, place names and arrows describe the specific sample artwork; they do not establish current conditions at a real location.

Coverage: **263 selected identifiers plus 235 linked pages = 498 records**. There are **494 eligible image candidates and 4 retained optional image holds**. Eligibility remains subject to the size and quality limits in the [asset manifest](sign-asset-manifest.json). These captions do not approve the final PDF.

Sources: Transportstyrelsen detail pages and primary Swedish legislation. Each entry links its specific page and, where needed, the parent meaning. The [structured captions](sign-captions.json) contain source dates, hashes, legal locators and exact asset bindings. See the [review and handoff](sign-caption-review.md) and [checks](sign-caption-checks.json).

Accessibility: use the supplied alternative text together with the teaching explanation. Still images cannot teach the timing of flashing lamps, sound or moving gestures. Held images have no approved alternative text. SIG identifiers are catalogue identifiers, not paragraph numbers in legislation. M19a and M20a have distinct meanings despite appearing as linked alternatives.

## A1 — Varning för farlig kurva

**English label (editorial):** Dangerous bend

A hazardous bend lies ahead. The symbol shows its direction; adjust speed before entering it.

**Image alternative text:** Dangerous bend. Left bend.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-3982a5bafb8cc0f5](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-farlig-kurva/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A1

## A10 — Varning för slirig väg

**English label (editorial):** Slippery road

The road may be slippery. Do not assume that the warning refers only to snow or ice.

**Image alternative text:** Slippery road. Skidding car inside a red-bordered yellow triangle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-f2e89de7c63b888f](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-slirig-vag/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A10

## A11 — Varning för stenskott

**English label (editorial):** Loose chippings

There is a risk of stones being thrown up. This warning is not normally used where that risk is routine, such as a gravel road.

**Image alternative text:** Loose chippings. Car wheel throws stones inside a warning triangle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-ee1053516fdea4a8](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-stenskott/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A11

## A13 — Varning för övergångsställe

**English label (editorial):** Pedestrian crossing ahead

Prepare to encounter a pedestrian crossing. Identify the crossing itself and the controls that apply there.

**Image alternative text:** Pedestrian crossing ahead. Pedestrian on crossing stripes inside a warning triangle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a4c7fce0b15a1ee4](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-overgangsstalle/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A13

## A28 — Varning för vägkorsning

**English label (editorial):** Junction with right-hand priority

The junction ahead is governed by the right-hand priority rule. Do not assume the broader road has priority.

**Image alternative text:** Junction with right-hand priority. Black cross inside a warning triangle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-84469dcf75d981ca](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-vagkorsning/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A28

## A29 — Varning för vägkorsning där trafikanter på anslutande väg har väjningsplikt eller stopplikt

**English label (editorial):** Junction with yielding side roads

Traffic joining from the connecting roads must yield or stop. Match the diagram to the actual junction; it does not remove your duty of care.

**Image alternative text:** Junction with yielding side roads. Thick straight road with a thin road joining from each side at right angles.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-6895cc22ad5c5db9](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-vagkorsning-dar-trafikanter-pa-anslutande-vag-har-vajningsplikt-eller-stopplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A29

Expect less road width ahead. The warning does not by itself decide priority over oncoming traffic.

**Image alternative text:** Road narrows. The road narrows on both sides.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-31d7b9a4321f0893](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-avsmalnande-vag/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A5

## A8 — Varning för ojämn väg

**English label (editorial):** Uneven road

Expect surface irregularities or damage that call for a lower speed.

**Image alternative text:** Uneven road. Uneven surface inside a warning triangle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7d7d3b141df69f6c](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-ojamn-vag/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A8

## A9 — Varning för farthinder

**English label (editorial):** Speed-control hump or dip

A deliberately raised or lowered part of the road is ahead. The graphic distinguishes the form.

**Image alternative text:** Speed-control hump or dip. Raised hump.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-b13b581ee06eddba](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/varningsmarken/varning-for-farthinder/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; A9

## B1 — Väjningsplikt

**English label (editorial):** Give way

Yield to vehicles on the crossing road or route. With a distance or stop-ahead panel, this can instead be advance information.

**Image alternative text:** Give way. Inverted triangular give-way sign.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-d1d38cce8664fe04](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/vajningsplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B1

## B2 — Stopplikt

**English label (editorial):** Stop

Make a complete stop before entering the crossing road, route or track area. Use the stop line; without one, apply the prescribed stopping position.

**Image alternative text:** Stop. Red octagon bearing STOP.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-e2fb3e8a84872721](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/stopplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B2

## B3 — Övergångsställe

**English label (editorial):** Pedestrian crossing

This identifies a pedestrian crossing. At an uncontrolled crossing, drivers yield to people who have entered or are about to enter.

**Image alternative text:** Pedestrian crossing. Pedestrian on crossing stripes within a white triangle on blue.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-03df3ee4f3495774](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/overgangsstalle/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B3; [SFS-1998-1276](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/trafikforordning-19981276_sfs-1998-1276/) — 3 kap. 61 §

## B4 — Huvudled

**English label (editorial):** Priority road

A priority road begins or continues. Still check the actual junction, controls and other road users.

**Image alternative text:** Priority road. Yellow diamond inside a white border.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-b56155e1f6c557da](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/huvudled/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B4

## B5 — Huvudled upphör

**English label (editorial):** End of priority road

The priority-road designation ends. Assess the rules at the next junction afresh.

**Image alternative text:** End of priority road. Priority-road diamond crossed by a black diagonal band.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-56bf7121b2b13526](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/huvudled-upphor/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B5

## B6 — Väjningsplikt mot mötande trafik

**English label (editorial):** Give way to oncoming traffic

Yield to oncoming vehicles at the restricted section.

**Image alternative text:** Give way to oncoming traffic. Upward red arrow beside a downward black arrow.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-0e255032a4a51e5b](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/vajningsplikt-mot-motande-trafik/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B6

## B7 — Mötande trafik har väjningsplikt

**English label (editorial):** Oncoming traffic must give way

Oncoming vehicles have the yielding duty. Do not force passage when it would be unsafe.

**Image alternative text:** Oncoming traffic must give way. Upward white arrow beside a downward red arrow on blue.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a62a2bf723474676](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/motande-trafik-har-vajningsplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B7

## B8 — Cykelöverfart

**English label (editorial):** Cycle crossing

This identifies a cycle crossing. Drivers yield to cyclists and class II moped riders on or about to enter it; riders must also consider approaching vehicles.

**Image alternative text:** Cycle crossing. Cyclist above a crossing in a blue square.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-61afce2d894a394b](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vajningspliktsmarken/cykeloverfart/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; B8; [SFS-1998-1276](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/trafikforordning-19981276_sfs-1998-1276/) — 3 kap. 61 a §; 6 kap. 6 §

## C1 — Förbud mot infart med fordon

**English label (editorial):** No entry

Vehicles must not enter from this direction. Do not confuse this with a prohibition applying in both directions.

**Image alternative text:** No entry. Red circle with a horizontal yellow bar.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7ec1b4a60cb361ee](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-infart-med-fordon/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C1

## C16 — Begränsad fordonsbredd

**English label (editorial):** Maximum vehicle width

Vehicles wider than the displayed limit may not pass. Assess the actual width, including the load.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum vehicle width. Width 2.2 metres.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-65aeb1903b828241](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransad-fordonsbredd/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C16

## C17 — Begränsad fordonshöjd

**English label (editorial):** Maximum vehicle height

Vehicles higher than the displayed limit may not pass. The sign is used for restricted clearance below 4.5 metres.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum vehicle height. Height 3.5 metres.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-cd868fad25254b4e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransad-fordonshojd/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C17

## C18 — Begränsad fordonslängd

**English label (editorial):** Maximum vehicle or combination length

Do not pass with a vehicle or combination longer than the displayed limit.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum vehicle or combination length. Length 20 metres, with a vehicle-and-trailer pictogram.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-05f5beaefdf7d2eb](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransad-fordonslangd/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C18

## C19 — Minsta avstånd

**English label (editorial):** Minimum following distance

Keep at least the displayed distance from a motor vehicle ahead travelling in the same direction.

**Image alternative text:** Minimum following distance. 50 metres between two vehicle symbols.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-ba3868b928709d64](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/minsta-avstand/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C19

## C2 — Förbud mot trafik med fordon

**English label (editorial):** No vehicles

Vehicle traffic is prohibited. Read any applicable supplementary qualifications.

**Image alternative text:** No vehicles. Yellow circle with a red border.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-2a4e782b3bbc7729](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-trafik-med-fordon/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C2

## C20 — Begränsad bruttovikt på fordon

**English label (editorial):** Maximum gross weight of a vehicle

The displayed value limits the vehicle's actual gross weight, not its registered maximum weight.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum gross weight of a vehicle. 12 tonnes.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-0cbe0d3a9a492d46](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransad-bruttovikt-pa-fordon/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C20

## C21 — Begränsad bruttovikt på fordon och fordonståg

**English label (editorial):** Maximum gross weight of a vehicle or combination

The displayed value limits the actual gross weight of the vehicle or combination.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum gross weight of a vehicle or combination. 30 tonnes with a vehicle-and-trailer pictogram.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-17ab5e2d99be68bd](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransad-bruttovikt-pa-fordon-och-fordonstag/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C21

## C22 — Bärighetsklass

**English label (editorial):** Road bearing-capacity class

The displayed class identifies the road's bearing-capacity regime. Check the applicable axle and weight limits for your vehicle.

**Image alternative text:** Road bearing-capacity class. BK3, the displayed bearing-capacity class.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-8575e72a8dc061f2](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/barighetsklass/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C22

## C23 — Begränsat axeltryck

**English label (editorial):** Maximum axle load

Do not exceed the displayed load on an axle.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum axle load. 6 tonnes on an axle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-01f8a4858629d550](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransat-axeltryck/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C23

## C24 — Begränsat boggitryck

**English label (editorial):** Maximum bogie load

The displayed limit applies to the bogie load, not the weight of the whole vehicle.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum bogie load. 10 tonnes on a bogie.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-73f883fb0321b63e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/begransat-boggitryck/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C24

## C25 — Förbud mot sväng i korsning

**English label (editorial):** Prohibited turn

The depicted turn is prohibited at the relevant junction. A length panel can extend the restriction across several junctions.

**Image alternative text:** Prohibited turn. Left-turn arrow crossed out.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-181296f6f797c632](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-svang-i-korsning/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C25

## C26 — Förbud mot U-sväng

**English label (editorial):** No U-turn

A U-turn is prohibited at the relevant junction or location. Read any panel extending the restriction.

**Image alternative text:** No U-turn. U-turn arrow crossed out.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-233b8f6499f734df](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-u-svang/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C26

## C27 — Förbud mot omkörning

**English label (editorial):** No overtaking

Do not overtake motor vehicles other than two-wheeled mopeds and motorcycles without sidecars. Other overtaking safety rules still apply; the sign restriction continues to C28.

**Image alternative text:** No overtaking. Red and black cars side by side.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-3db6f1168c00d607](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-omkorning/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C27

## C28 — Slut på förbud mot omkörning

**English label (editorial):** End of overtaking prohibition

The prohibition introduced by C27 ends. General overtaking restrictions still apply.

**Image alternative text:** End of overtaking prohibition. Overtaking symbol crossed by diagonal black lines.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-89d1c4b87308f7e9](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/slut-pa-forbud-mot-omkorning/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C28

## C3 — Förbud mot trafik med annat motordrivet fordon än moped klass II

**English label (editorial):** No motor vehicles except class II mopeds

Motor vehicles are prohibited, with class II mopeds excluded unless a panel also prohibits them.

**Image alternative text:** No motor vehicles except class II mopeds. Car and motorcycle crossed by a red diagonal.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-5cabc3ff99926170](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-trafik-med-annat-motordrivet-fordon-an-moped-klass-ii/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C3

## C31 — Hastighetsbegränsning

**English label (editorial):** Maximum speed

Do not exceed the displayed speed in km/h. A junction alone does not necessarily end the limit, and conditions may require a lower speed.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum speed. Red-bordered yellow circle displaying 30: maximum 30 km/h.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7e6307e67eababde](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/hastighetsbegransning/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C31

## C32 — Tillfällig hastighetsbegränsning upphör

**English label (editorial):** End of temporary speed limit

The temporary signed speed limit ends. Establish the speed limit that now applies rather than assuming there is none.

**Image alternative text:** End of temporary speed limit. 30 crossed by diagonal black lines: temporary 30 km/h limit ends.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-d74a04fcabf2ee16](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/tillfallig-hastighetsbegransning-upphor/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C32

## C35 — Förbud mot att parkera fordon

**English label (editorial):** No parking

Parking is prohibited on the signed side for the applicable extent. This does not make stopping lawful where another rule prohibits it.

**Image alternative text:** No parking. Single red diagonal across a blue circle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-4015194fac3d001e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-att-parkera-fordon/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C35

## C36 — Förbud mot att parkera fordon på dag med udda datum

**English label (editorial):** No parking on odd-numbered dates

The parking prohibition applies on dates with an odd number. Read the time and extent qualifications as well.

**Image alternative text:** No parking on odd-numbered dates. Single upright stroke on the no-parking symbol: odd-numbered dates.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-3d49ab25ddd317aa](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-att-parkera-fordon-pa-dag-med-udda-datum/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C36

## C37 — Förbud mot att parkera fordon på dag med jämnt datum

**English label (editorial):** No parking on even-numbered dates

The parking prohibition applies on dates with an even number. Read the time and extent qualifications as well.

**Image alternative text:** No parking on even-numbered dates. Two upright strokes on the no-parking symbol: even-numbered dates.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-bc38387e084fd29e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-att-parkera-fordon-pa-dag-med-jamnt-datum/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C37

## C38 — Datumparkering

**English label (editorial):** Date-dependent parking prohibition

On even dates, do not park on the side with even address numbers; on odd dates, do not park on the odd-numbered side. Check any time conditions.

**Image alternative text:** Date-dependent parking prohibition. Odd/even-date symbols divided by a red diagonal.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a1a0f160353ada9e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/datumparkering/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C38

## C39 — Förbud mot att stanna och parkera fordon

**English label (editorial):** No stopping or parking

Both stopping and parking are prohibited for the indicated extent. Waiting with the engine running is not a general exemption.

**Image alternative text:** No stopping or parking. Red cross on a blue circle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7c30232c8f10cf70](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/forbud-mot-att-stanna-och-parkera-fordon/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C39

## C40 — Ändamålsplats

**English label (editorial):** Place reserved for a stated purpose

Read the stated purpose. A loading place concerns loading or unloading heavy or bulky goods, not unrestricted waiting.

**Image alternative text:** Place reserved for a stated purpose. Lastplats: loading place.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-64b499f750bd6262](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/forbudsmarken/andamalsplats/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; C40

## C41 — Slut på ändamålsplats

**English label (editorial):** End of reserved-purpose place

The place reserved for the stated purpose ends.

## C42 — Vändplats

**English label (editorial):** Turning area

This space is intended for turning and parking is prohibited. If a no-stopping symbol is inserted, stopping is prohibited too.

The signed turning area ends.

## C44 — Förbud mot trafik med annat motordrivet fordon med dubbdäck än moped klass II

**English label (editorial):** No studded-tyre motor vehicles except class II mopeds

The prohibition concerns motor vehicles with studded tyres. Class II mopeds are included only if a supplementary panel says so.

**English label (editorial):** Combined stopping or parking restriction

Read the inserted prohibition and its times together with the sign above. This panel can qualify or vary that sign; artwork variants are not interchangeable.

## M1 — Mittlinje eller körfältslinje

**English label (editorial):** Centre or lane line

A broken line separates opposing directions or adjacent lanes, depending on its position. It does not guarantee that crossing or overtaking is safe.

**Image alternative text:** Centre or lane line. Two examples of a broken white line on a dark road background.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-1153dc81ff6b7855](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/mittlinje-eller-korfaltslinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M1

## M10 — Mittlinje eller körfältslinje och heldragen linje

**English label (editorial):** Broken line beside a continuous line

The continuous line governs a driver on its side of the marking. Do not treat the broken line on the other side as permission to change lanes.

**Image alternative text:** Broken line beside a continuous line. Broken white line beside a continuous line.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-e23dfb819cedc45d](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/mittlinje-eller-korfaltslinje-och-heldragen-linje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M10

## M11 — Varningslinje och heldragen linje

**English label (editorial):** Warning line beside a continuous line

The continuous-line side restricts lane changes; the warning-line side indicates that crossing is unsuitable.

**Image alternative text:** Warning line beside a continuous line. Warning line beside a continuous line.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-6c26b7a07a7cd5c3](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/varningslinje-och-heldragen-linje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M11

## M12 — Mittlinje och varningslinje

**English label (editorial):** Centre line beside a warning line

A driver on the warning-line side should recognise that crossing is unsuitable because of the conditions.

**Image alternative text:** Centre line beside a warning line. Broken centre line beside a warning line.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-2cda1dbb11d58136](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/mittlinje-och-varningslinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M12

## M13 — Stopplinje

**English label (editorial):** Stop line

This identifies where to stop when a sign or signal requires it. The line alone does not create a universal stop duty.

**Image alternative text:** Stop line. Wide transverse white stop line.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-1fdb585fabaae5a3](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/stopplinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M13

## M14 — Väjningslinje

**English label (editorial):** Give-way line

This identifies the line drivers should not pass while yielding.

**Image alternative text:** Give-way line. Row of white give-way triangles.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7be9c2f5fdee4e9e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/vajningslinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M14

## M15 — Övergångsställe

**English label (editorial):** Pedestrian crossing marking

This marks the pedestrian crossing associated with B3. Identify whether it is signal-controlled before applying the crossing duties.

**Image alternative text:** Pedestrian crossing marking. Parallel white crossing stripes.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-54d241bde05e72a0](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/overgangsstalle/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M15

## M16 — Cykelpassage eller cykelöverfart

**English label (editorial):** Cycle passage or cycle crossing marking

The blocks alone do not identify a cycle crossing. A cycle crossing also has B8 and the give-way marking M14; assess the controls and your movement.

**Image alternative text:** Cycle passage or cycle crossing marking. Two parallel rows of white blocks marking the sides of a cycle passage or crossing.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-c2367de89f1f6d63](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/cykelpassage-eller-cykeloverfart/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M16

## M18 — Förberedande upplysning om väjningsplikt eller stopplikt

**English label (editorial):** Give way or stop ahead marking

Prepare for a yielding or stopping duty further ahead in your direction.

**Image alternative text:** Give way or stop ahead marking. White inverted triangle painted on the road.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a676ad9a65c6be7b](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/forberedande-upplysning-om-vajningsplikt-eller-stopplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M18

## M19 — Körfältspilar

**English label (editorial):** Lane arrows

Choose the appropriate lane for the next junction. Where a continuous line bounds the lane, follow its arrow directions, subject to the specified reserved-lane exception.

**Image alternative text:** Lane arrows. Examples of straight, branching and turning lane arrows.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-016f28a7da8ccc18](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/korfaltspilar/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M19

## M2 — Kantlinje

**English label (editorial):** Edge line

This broken line marks the outside edge of the carriageway.

**Image alternative text:** Edge line. Short broken white edge line.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-c40cf57a54f235a1](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/kantlinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M2

## M20 — Körfältsbyte

**English label (editorial):** Change-lane arrow

A lane change is needed promptly. Check that the manoeuvre can be made safely.

**Image alternative text:** Change-lane arrow. White change-lane arrow bending sideways.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-ec264fe3ab7daa24](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/korfaltsbyte/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M20

## M21 — Förbud mot att stanna och parkera

**English label (editorial):** No stopping or parking edge marking

The continuous yellow edge marking indicates a stopping and parking prohibition.

**Image alternative text:** No stopping or parking edge marking. Continuous yellow line at the road edge.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7ed59a4af05a0b0b](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/forbud-mot-att-stanna-och-parkera/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M21

## M22 — Förbud mot att parkera

**English label (editorial):** No parking edge marking

The broken yellow edge marking indicates a parking prohibition; it can also show the extent of a bus stop.

**Image alternative text:** No parking edge marking. Broken yellow line at the road edge.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-d28db067f2158149](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/forbud-mot-att-parkera/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M22

## M23 — Förbud mot att stanna och parkera eller att parkera

**English label (editorial):** Reinforcement of a stopping or parking restriction

This yellow marking reinforces the applicable M21 or M22 restriction. Identify which prohibition applies.

**Image alternative text:** Reinforcement of a stopping or parking restriction. Yellow zigzag marking reinforcing a stopping or parking restriction.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-03cd02fda6bffbf0](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/forbud-mot-att-stanna-och-parkera-eller-att-parkera/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M23

## M24 — Uppställningsplats

**English label (editorial):** Parking-space boundary

This marks the limits of a space for a vehicle; the boundary may be broken rather than continuous.

**Image alternative text:** Parking-space boundary. White outline of a parking space.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-c765a3aac06baa16](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/uppstallningsplats/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M24

## M25 — Gång- och cykelpil

**English label (editorial):** Pedestrian or cycle direction arrow

This shows a suitable route for pedestrians, cyclists or class II moped riders, according to context.

**Image alternative text:** Pedestrian or cycle direction arrow. White arrow points right.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-787cb861d5b7060d](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/gang--och-cykelpil/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M25

## M26 — Cykel

**English label (editorial):** Cycle route marking

This identifies a path or suitable route for bicycles and class II mopeds.

**Image alternative text:** Cycle route marking. White bicycle road marking.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-24aaa8ee986c7773](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/cykel/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M26

## M27 — Gående

**English label (editorial):** Pedestrian route marking

This identifies a path or suitable route for pedestrians.

**Image alternative text:** Pedestrian route marking. White adult and child road marking.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-120af32d36eb2c69](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/gaende/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M27

## M3 — Varningslinje

**English label (editorial):** Warning line

Crossing this line is unsuitable because of the traffic conditions. It may also warn of a continuous line ahead.

**Image alternative text:** Warning line. Long white strokes with short gaps.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-2e89e60ce4156886](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/varningslinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M3

## M33 — Rörelsehindrad

**English label (editorial):** Accessible parking marking

This marks a space reserved for people with mobility impairments. Check the applicable permit conditions.

**Image alternative text:** Accessible parking marking. Wheelchair symbol painted on the road.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-fad5c65bef7fd5ce](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/rorelsehindrad/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M33

## M4 — Ledlinje

**English label (editorial):** Guidance line

Follow the appropriate guidance through a complex junction or another place where the route needs clarification.

**Image alternative text:** Guidance line. Short white guidance dashes.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-751a3f3541d81798](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/ledlinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M4

## M5 — Cykelfältslinje

**English label (editorial):** Cycle-lane boundary

This marks the boundary between a cycle lane and another lane.

**Image alternative text:** Cycle-lane boundary. Closely spaced white cycle-lane boundary strokes.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-78b644f48a3925eb](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/cykelfaltslinje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M5

## M6 — Linje för fordon i linjetrafik med flera

**English label (editorial):** Public-transport lane boundary

This marks a lane for scheduled public transport and other permitted users. Check who may use it before entering.

**Image alternative text:** Public-transport lane boundary. Wide broken white lane boundary.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-3f5bda80a1108fd5](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/linje-for-fordon-i-linjetrafik-med-flera/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M6

## M7 — Reversibelt körfält

**English label (editorial):** Reversible-lane boundary

The lane can carry traffic in different directions at different times. Its current control determines whether you may use it.

**Image alternative text:** Reversible-lane boundary. Paired broken white lane boundaries.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-cfc706eaa4c8068d](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/reversibelt-korfalt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M7

## M8 — Heldragen linje

**English label (editorial):** Continuous line

Crossing restrictions apply according to the type of continuous line and the traffic rules. Do not invent an exception merely because the road appears clear.

**Image alternative text:** Continuous line. Single and double continuous white lines.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-4d5f37e4eacf9b66](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/heldragen-linje/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M8; [SFS-1998-1276](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/trafikforordning-19981276_sfs-1998-1276/) — 3 kap. 11 §

## M9 — Spärrområde

**English label (editorial):** Hatched area

Vehicles must not normally enter the marked area. Only the specified traffic-rule exceptions can justify crossing it.

**Image alternative text:** Hatched area. White hatched area on a dark road background.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-220101247b078200](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/vagmarkeringar/sparromrade/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 4 kap.; M9; [SFS-1998-1276](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/trafikforordning-19981276_sfs-1998-1276/) — 3 kap. 11 §

## P1 — Stopp

**English label (editorial):** Police stop instruction — front and rear

Traffic approaching the officer from the front or rear must stop; traffic approaching from the sides may proceed. The officer's continuing orientation matters even after the arm movement.

**Image alternative text:** Police stop instruction — front and rear. Officer faces the viewer with one arm extended sideways.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-95c55b6eac31ee91](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/stopp/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P1

## P10 — Följ efter och stanna bakom polisfordonet när detta stannar

**English label (editorial):** Follow and stop behind the police vehicle

The vehicle behind must follow the police vehicle and stop when it stops.

**Image alternative text:** Follow and stop behind the police vehicle. Police vehicle with officer's paddle signalling a following vehicle.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a6a91dc4bb280885](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/folj-efter-och-stanna-bakom-polisfordonet-nar-detta-stannar/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P10

## P11 — Kör in till vägkanten och stanna framför polisfordonet

**English label (editorial):** Pull over and stop ahead of the police vehicle

The driver in front of the police vehicle must pull to the road edge and stop. Alternating blue and red lights convey this instruction.

**Image alternative text:** Pull over and stop ahead of the police vehicle. Police vehicle with red and blue lights; alternation is represented by a still drawing.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-f17e57c4f38a3616](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/kor-in-till-vagkanten-och-stanna-framfor-polisfordonet/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P11

## P2 — Stopp

**English label (editorial):** Police stop instruction — palm facing you

Stop if the officer's palm faces you. A stop paddle may be used instead.

**Image alternative text:** Police stop instruction — palm facing you. Officer raises a palm towards approaching traffic.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-b65a68982e70dd92](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/stopp1/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P2

## P3 — Stopp

**English label (editorial):** Police stop instruction — light facing you

Stop if the officer's lamp faces you. This instruction is used in darkness or reduced visibility.

**Image alternative text:** Police stop instruction — light facing you. Officer holds a red light towards approaching traffic.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-03ad9b053bd491f2](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/stopp2/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P3

## P4 — Kör fram

**English label (editorial):** Police instruction to proceed

Traffic in the indicated direction may proceed. The officer gives a waving movement in that direction; the still image does not show the whole movement.

**Image alternative text:** Police instruction to proceed. Officer gestures sideways; motion arrows depict the wave to proceed.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-5981d190ea7ae78e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/kor-fram/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P4

## P5 — Minska hastigheten

**English label (editorial):** Police instruction to slow down

The road user facing the officer must reduce speed. A lamp or reflector may reinforce the instruction in poor visibility.

**Image alternative text:** Police instruction to slow down. Officer extends an arm with motion arrows depicting an up-and-down slowing gesture.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-c830ec9e4c2b6225](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/minska-hastigheten/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P5

## P6 — Kontroll

**English label (editorial):** Police inspection point

Enter the inspection site as directed. If vehicle symbols are displayed, the instruction applies to those vehicle groups.

**Image alternative text:** Police inspection point. Kontroll, goods-vehicle symbol and arrow pointing up-right.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-70620efb1f305dfe](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/kontroll/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P6

## P7 — Förberedande upplysning om kontroll

**English label (editorial):** Police inspection ahead

This gives advance information about a police inspection point.

**Image alternative text:** Police inspection ahead. Kontroll, goods-vehicle symbol, 1000 m and an arrow bending left.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-91e21d6690f09bc4](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/forberedande-upplysning-om-kontroll/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P7

## P8 — Minska hastigheten

**English label (editorial):** Slow down behind the police vehicle

Traffic behind the police vehicle must reduce speed in response to this instruction.

**Image alternative text:** Slow down behind the police vehicle. Police vehicle with an arm extended and motion arrows signalling slowing to following traffic.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-45cfc5f8c0e1e882](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/minska-hastigheten1/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P8

## P9 — Minska hastigheten

**English label (editorial):** Slow down when approaching the police vehicle

Traffic meeting the police vehicle must reduce speed in response to this instruction.

**Image alternative text:** Slow down when approaching the police vehicle. Police vehicle with a waving arm signalling slowing to oncoming traffic.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-1157bca6273a7bb9](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tecken-av-polisman-med-flera/minska-hastigheten2/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 7 kap.; P9

This symbol identifies a släpkärra. Read its role from the surrounding sign or panel.

## SIG1 — Röd

**English label (editorial):** Red vehicle signal

Stop before the stop line or, if none, before the signal. An outline arrow limits the indication to the direction shown.

**Image alternative text:** Red vehicle signal. Red left arrow, red full lamp and red bicycle signal examples.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-a1b956d66e31bc68](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/rod/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG10 — Lodrätt streck eller pil

**English label (editorial):** Public-transport signal — vertical bar or arrow

The controlled vehicle or tram may proceed. An arrow applies to its indicated direction.

**Image alternative text:** Public-transport signal — vertical bar or arrow. White vertical bar in the lower signal lamp.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-87ef111c015c825c](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/lodratt-streck-eller-pil/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG11 — Vågrätt streck

**English label (editorial):** Public-transport signal — horizontal bar

Stop unless the change from the vertical indication occurs so late that stopping would be dangerous. This is not the proceed indication.

**Image alternative text:** Public-transport signal — horizontal bar. White horizontal bar in the middle signal lamp.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7db8df330506dc33](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/vagratt-streck/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG12 — Rött kryss

**English label (editorial):** Lane-control red cross

The lane and, where applicable, its adjacent shoulder must not be used for traffic.

**Image alternative text:** Lane-control red cross. Red cross on black.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-bf220032791bd7cc](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/rott-kryss/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG13 — Gul pil eller pilar

**English label (editorial):** Lane-control amber arrow

Change lane in an indicated direction, making the manoeuvre safely.

**Image alternative text:** Lane-control amber arrow. Amber arrows point diagonally down to both sides in one example and down-right in another.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-78c24d7a00bf05ec](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/gul-pil-eller-pilar/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG14 — Grön pil

**English label (editorial):** Lane-control green arrow

The lane may be used. Other traffic restrictions and safety duties still apply.

**Image alternative text:** Lane-control green arrow. Green arrow points down.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-f3d8497363eaf993](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/gron-pil/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG2 — Röd + gul

**English label (editorial):** Red and amber vehicle signal

Green is about to follow, but the stop instruction still applies. Do not start merely because amber has appeared.

**Image alternative text:** Red and amber vehicle signal. Red and amber illuminated together in arrow, full-lamp and bicycle examples.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-40769957401954c7](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/rod--gul/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG3 — Grön

**English label (editorial):** Green vehicle signal

You may proceed subject to the remaining traffic duties. A green arrow applies to its direction, including when another signal indication appears simultaneously.

**Image alternative text:** Green vehicle signal. Green arrow, green full lamp and green bicycle signal examples.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-0a0e509daa0e15e5](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/gron/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG4 — Gul

**English label (editorial):** Amber vehicle signal

Stop, unless the change from green occurs when stopping would be dangerous because you are already too close. An outline arrow limits the indication to its direction.

**Image alternative text:** Amber vehicle signal. Amber arrow, amber full lamp and amber bicycle signal examples.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-0e4e11362af0c997](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/gul/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG5 — Blinkande gul

**English label (editorial):** Flashing amber vehicle signal

Proceed with particular caution and apply the other instructions and traffic rules. Flashing is not demonstrated by the still image.

**Image alternative text:** Flashing amber vehicle signal. Amber flashing lamp represented by a still drawing.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-54b72a2183539993](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/blinkande-gul/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG6 — Röd akustisk signal med långsam pulsfrekvens

**English label (editorial):** Red pedestrian signal with slow audible pulses

Do not begin crossing. If already crossing when it changes to red, continue to the other side or stop on a refuge where one exists.

**Image alternative text:** Red pedestrian signal with slow audible pulses. Red standing pedestrian; audible pulses are not reproduced.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-e08ca782028734bc](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/rod-akustisk-signal-med-langsam-pulsfrekvens/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG7 — Grön akustisk signal med snabb pulsfrekvens

**English label (editorial):** Green pedestrian signal with fast audible pulses

Pedestrians may begin crossing. The still image does not reproduce the audible pulse pattern.

**Image alternative text:** Green pedestrian signal with fast audible pulses. Green walking pedestrian; audible pulses are not reproduced.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-9847faafc8dedb4e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/gron-akustisk-signal-med-snabb-pulsfrekvens/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG8 — S

**English label (editorial):** Public-transport signal — S

Stop before the line or, without one, before the signal. This is a signal for the traffic it controls, not a general car signal.

**Image alternative text:** Public-transport signal — S. White S in the upper lamp of a public-transport signal.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-7719a02329d02642](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/s/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## SIG9 — S+vågrätt streck

**English label (editorial):** Public-transport signal — S and horizontal bar

A proceed indication is about to follow, but the stop instruction remains in force.

**Image alternative text:** Public-transport signal — S and horizontal bar. White S and horizontal bar lit together.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-f0ce1f783c39f420](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/trafiksignaler/svagratt-streck/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 3 kap.; matching named signal indication (SIG is a website identifier)

## T1 — Vägsträckas längd

**English label (editorial):** Length of affected road section

The panel identifies the beginning and end of the affected section. Distinguish this from a distance to a single feature.

**Image alternative text:** Two official outline distance-range examples: 200–800 m and 0–1,2 km.

**Status:** Eligible as exact official poster vector at the recorded 80/110 mm review width. Earlier individual-file hold retained in history.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-be91c91e0e3fc544](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/vagstrackas-langd/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T1

## T10 — Nedsatt hörsel

**English label (editorial):** People with impaired hearing

People with impaired hearing commonly use the area. Do not assume a horn will be heard.

**Image alternative text:** People with impaired hearing. Three black dots inside a black circle on yellow.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-8fb1796efe1777ee](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/nedsatt-horsel/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T10

## T11 — Utsträckning

**English label (editorial):** Extent of the instruction

Two-ended arrows indicate both sides of the sign; a downward arrow marks the end; a left or right arrow indicates that direction. Read the exact form and context.

**Image alternative text:** Extent of the instruction. Black arrows point left and right.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

Traffic on the thin-line roads yields to traffic on the thick-line road. Follow the actual road layout shown.

**English label (editorial):** Parking fee

Pay according to the stated tariff and follow the other conditions at the payment facility. Read any time qualification.

**Image alternative text:** Parking fee. Text Avgift: fee.

## T20 — Parkeringsbiljett

Parking is free, but a parking ticket must be used. Do not interpret the ticket symbol as a fee requirement.

Read the text as a qualification of the sign above; it supplies information not expressed by the other panels.

## T24 — Laddplats

**English label (editorial):** Parking for externally chargeable vehicles

Below E19, only vehicles capable of external electric charging may park. The symbol alone does not say that charging must be in progress; check all posted conditions.

**Image alternative text:** Parking for externally chargeable vehicles. Plug-in vehicle symbol.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

## T3 — Avstånd till stopplikt

**English label (editorial):** Distance to a stop requirement

Used below B1, this provides advance notice of a stop requirement at the indicated distance. It is not an instruction to stop at this advance sign.

**Image alternative text:** Distance to a stop requirement. STOPP 200 m: a stop requirement 200 metres ahead.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-ffec90718f2d183e](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/avstand-till-stopplikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T3

## T4 — Fri bredd

**English label (editorial):** Available width

Read the stated clear width together with the main sign. It is not permission to exceed a separate vehicle-width limit.

**Image alternative text:** Available width. Available width 3.5 metres.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-9fc413a0bb0edf88](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/fri-bredd/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T4

## T5 — Totalvikt

**English label (editorial):** Maximum authorised weight threshold

The main sign applies to vehicles whose registered maximum authorised weight (totalvikt) exceeds this value. Do not confuse it with actual gross weight.

**Image alternative text:** Maximum authorised weight threshold. Maximum authorised weight threshold 3.5 tonnes.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-be2d8ef2d103d781](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/totalvikt/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T5

## T6 — Tidsangivelse

**English label (editorial):** Time qualification

Unbracketed black/white times apply on weekdays other than the day before a Sunday or public holiday; brackets mean those preceding days; red means Sundays and public holidays. A named day governs regardless of its category. A period crossing midnight continues into the next day.

**Image alternative text:** Official time-panel example: outline 8–17, bracketed outline (8–14), and red 8–13.

**Status:** Eligible as exact official poster vector at the recorded 80/110 mm review width. Earlier individual-file hold retained in history.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-5cf2b08238df71da](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/tillaggstavlor/tidsangivelse/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 2 kap.; T6

**English label (editorial):** Accessible-parking permit holders

Below E19, the parking instruction applies to users entitled to park with the specified accessible-parking permit. Read any time limits too.

**Image alternative text:** Accessible-parking permit holders. White wheelchair symbol on blue.

The sign above applies only to the vehicle or road-user group shown on the panel. Read time qualifications as part of the same instruction.

## T9 — Nedsatt syn

**English label (editorial):** People with impaired vision

People with impaired vision commonly use the area. Give them time and space without assuming they have seen you.

**Image alternative text:** People with impaired vision. Five black dots arranged horizontally on yellow.

## V1 — Stopp

**English label (editorial):** Traffic guard — stop

Stop if the guard faces you. A red lamp is used in darkness; an authorised stop sign marked Vakt may also be used.

**Image alternative text:** Traffic guard — stop. Guard faces approaching traffic and holds a red flag sideways.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

## V2 — Kör fram

**English label (editorial):** Traffic guard — proceed

Proceed in the direction indicated by the guard's waving movement. A still image does not show the full gesture.

**Image alternative text:** Traffic guard — proceed. Guard gestures to traffic; the complete movement is not reproduced in this still.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

## V3 — Kännetecken

The guard identifier must be worn on the front and back. It identifies the role rather than giving a separate movement instruction.

**Image alternative text:** Traffic guard identifier. Garment identification panel bearing VAKT.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

## X1 — Markeringspil

**English label (editorial):** Sharp-change direction marker

Turn sharply in the direction of the yellow arrow or arrows. Temporary use, including roadworks, has a red background.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**Official evidence:** [TS-SIGN-ce5b51042e6e2020](https://www.transportstyrelsen.se/sv/vagtrafik/trafikregler-och-vagmarken/vagmarken/andra-anordningar-for-anvisningar-for-trafiken/markeringspil/) — Official heading, explanation and artwork; [SFS-2007-90](https://www.riksdagen.se/sv/dokument-och-lagar/dokument/svensk-forfattningssamling/vagmarkesforordning-200790_sfs-2007-90/) — 5 kap.; X1

## X10 — Stolpmarkeringsanordning

This makes the supporting sign or traffic device easier to see. It is not a separate destination or priority instruction.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**English label (editorial):** Obstruction board

An obstruction restricts the road, which may be partly or fully closed. Temporary boards have a red background.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.

**English label (editorial):** Side-obstruction marker

Pass on the side towards which the yellow diagonal stripes slope down. A temporary horizontal-striped version permits passage on either side.

## X4 — Avfartsskärm

**English label (editorial):** Exit separation marker

This marks the split between the main carriageway and an exit with a deceleration lane.

**Image alternative text:** Exit separation marker. White upward chevrons on blue identify an exit separation.

Pass on a side indicated by the yellow illuminated arrow or arrows. The arrows may flash.

## X8 — Tillfällig stängning

**English label (editorial):** Temporary cancellation of direction information

The crossed-out destination instruction is temporarily not valid. Read the replacement route guidance.

## M19a — Körfältspil för cirkulationsplatser

**English label (editorial):** Roundabout lane arrow

On the approach to a roundabout, this marking helps you choose a suitable lane for the next junction and your onward route. Its layout is adapted to the location.

**Status:** Eligible within recorded asset limits.
